# ::id Amr/0157.test
# ::snt Türkiye Cumhuriyeti Milli Eğitim Bakanlığı Kadıköy Kız Yüksek Meslek Okulu Müdürlüğü
(n1/name
	:UNKNOWN_REL (n2/name)
	:UNKNOWN_REL -
	:UNKNOWN_REL (n3/name)
	:UNKNOWN_REL (g1/government-organization)
	:UNKNOWN_REL (n4/name)
	:UNKNOWN_REL -
	:UNKNOWN_REL (g2/government-organization
		:wiki -
		:name (n5/name
			:op1 (g3/government-organization 
				:name n5
				:wiki -)
			:op1 (1/türkiye)
			:op2 (2/cumhuriyet)
			:op3 (3/milli)
			:op4 (4/eğitim)
			:op5 (5/bakanlık)
			:op1 (6/kadıköy)
			:op2 (s1/school
				:wiki -
				:name n5
				:poss g2)
			:op1 (p1/province
				:wiki -
				:name n5)
			:op2 (7/kız)
			:op3 (8/yüksek)
			:op4 (9/meslek-okul)
			:op1 (11/müdürlük))
		:name n5
		:wiki -))

		:op6 (10/okul)
		:op1 (6/kadıköy)
		:op1 (1/türkiye)
		:op2 (2/cumhuriyeti)
		:op3 (3/milli)
		:op4 (4/eğitim)

	:UNKNOWN_REL (n2/name)
	:UNKNOWN_REL (n3/name))
